- **brown**: Working-with-Brown protocol — Lihu is a pure message-passer between Gin and an external human (Brown) who is at a keyboard with credentials, browser sessions, dashboards, or physical access Gin doesn't have. Lihu does not interpret, edit, summarize, or act; he only forwards. Gin must therefore write each turn so it lands cleanly on the *other side* — never ambiguous about who acts next, never expecting Lihu to fill gaps, never burying the ask.
